# PING Social outreach learnings

## 2026-09-08 — Round 1
- 7 prospects researched, 3 emails sent (MH Electrical & Solar, Sanders & Johnson, To The T), 1 form submitted (Green Mountain Landscaping).
- What went well: research-first personalization; Nolan rewrote the emails toward a conversational voice built on the PING principle (technology strengthens the worker and owner, never replaces their judgment). That is now the house voice. Briefer is better: round 2 drafts run about 120 words.
- Skipped: ARCM Roofing (their "contact form" is a customer quote wizard; submitting a fake quote request would be deceptive, never do that), Peak to Peak Roofing (image CAPTCHA needs a human), Just In Time (form requires a business street address, still waiting on Nolan).
- Watch: some prospect emails came from third-party listings rather than company sites, so treat them as unverified. Keep daily send volume modest to protect Gmail deliverability.

## 2026-09-09 — disclosure strengthened
- Nolan was getting automated "project estimate" responses because form messages land in estimate funnels. Auto-replies are acceptable (a human will read the message next), but the disclosure must be unmistakable: it is not a service request, no project, no estimate wanted, B2B message.
- New standing opening line (pitch.md base pitch updated): "Please read before replying: I am not a customer and this is not a service request. I am not looking to get any project done and I do not want an estimate. This is a business-to-business message from one business to another."
- Applied retroactively to all 6 queued round-7 email drafts (JB Flatwork, Super Junkers, Gone For Good, Fort Comfort Gutters, Majestic Painting, Certainty Appliance Repair) before they send on the 2026-09-10 UTC cap.
- Perpetual mode reaffirmed: engine runs forever until Nolan says stop.

## Round 95 (2026-09-19) send-pipeline reliability notes
- NEVER pipe the send script through `head` (or any truncating reader): `python3 send-queue.py 1 2>&1 | head -40` closed the pipe at 40 lines; slots 6-10 sent fine but their receipts never printed, and RC=0 was head's exit, not the script's. Completion state must come from Gmail sent-mail message IDs, not stdout. All 10 verified accepted via `users messages list` + metadata reads on the unprinted IDs.
- The `hatch_gws_cli gmail search` alias broke mid-run ("method not supported by the bundled schema") after working fine 30 minutes earlier; `users messages list --params '{"userId":"me","q":"..."}'` kept working. When the alias breaks, fall back to the raw users.messages.list path for send verification.
- +send needs no approval card on detached runs when the queue file carries the standing authorization; all 10 fired in ~90s with 8s spacing. (Historical note: round-13's queue stalled on a Gmail approval card, so this is not guaranteed; kill on stall per the queue file.)

## 2026-09-21 (round 105) — Ladder verification is per-recipient, never per-note (FL-20260921-030)
Two duplicate 4th touches went out because the leads.md ladder list still said "F2 eligible" while pipeline.md and the canonical JSONL correctly said "F2 SENT, ladder complete." The lesson that stuck: the ladder list is a planning artifact, not ground truth; ground truth is per-recipient sent-mail history (`in:sent to:<address>`), checked every time, right before the send. A combined OR-group Gmail search returned only 1 of the expected messages and that anomaly was walked past; from now on, any search result that contradicts the ladder files is a hard stop, never a green light. Stale draft files are now trashed the run their ladder completes (the round32-f2 drafts surviving 2 days past their F2s is what made the sends look ready). When files disagree, pipeline.md wins over leads.md, and sent mail wins over both.

## 2026-09-21 (round 106) — Keyword-only sent-mail searches are not proof of absence
The "miracle" keyword search returned nothing, which first looked like Miracle Maids never sent. The send was real; the recipient-address search (`in:sent gcapal`) returned the exact subject, address, and message-sent_at. Subjects never contain the business name. Lesson: per-recipient verification means searching the RECIPIENT ADDRESS, not a keyword. An empty keyword result is not evidence a send did not happen.

## Round 108 (2026-09-21 ~11:50 MDT) — Pig "unreachable" was a local-path mistake, not a Pig outage
Round-107 deferred PING emission with "Pig unreachable: ssh Operation not permitted." This round's diagnosis: a bare `ssh muse-ssh@100.79.154.43` bypasses the required proxy config, so it always fails. The configured path (`~/.ssh/pig-config` with `ProxyCommand nc -X connect -x hatch-egress-proxy:3130`, i.e. pigwsl-run.sh) succeeded immediately, and emit-observation.py already uses that same proxy path internally. Lesson: never declare the Pig unreachable from a bare ssh probe; test through the configured bridge first. A surface is BLOCKED only after the authorized path fails, not after an unauthorized one does.

The business-specific content was preserved; only voice mechanics changed. Lesson: any draft drafted before a voice-rule change must be re-voiced before firing; add a voice-compliance note to queue files when backfilling older drafts.

## Round 110 (2026-09-21 ~17:15 MDT) — Fire discipline: eligible at check time is not eligible at fire time
The 4 exit-0 cold slots (Grime Fighters, M1Pro Plumbing, GPS Plumbing & Mechanical, Shelton Drilling) were verified against a UTC-day cap that does not open until 18:00 MDT, a full 45 minutes after the gate check. Decision: nothing fires before the cap opens, even fully-eligible slots. Re-verification happens at fire time: fresh Gmail count for the current UTC day, then send, then confirm Gmail acceptance before marking SENT. Lesson: a send gate has an expiry; record the gate's timestamp and re-run the count immediately before firing when any UTC boundary or multi-minute gap intervenes.

## Round 115 — FL-20260922-003: send-batch.py parse drift (durable fix)

- Three latent parse defects in send-batch.py fired together on reconciliation-era drafts: (1) annotated To: lines ("To: addr (own-site footer email; ...)") fed the whole annotation string as the email, so the fire-time eligibility gate keyed on a non-address and fail-closed (Comfort Air F1 wrongly BLOCKED); (2) "- To:"/"- Subject:" markdown bullets and bullet metadata blocks plus "---" separators were not recognized as header, so older drafts parsed To/Subject as None; (3) trailing editorial notes after the sign-off line (e.g. "Re-voiced ...") were included in the message body. Fix: parse_draft strips parenthetical annotations, treats any leading "- " line / "---" / "#" / blank as metadata, starts the body at the first non-metadata line, and truncates the body at the sign-off line "PING Social, (970) 589-3309". Invariant going forward: a send script must round-trip its parsed (to, subject, body) through assertions (bare address with @, body starts at disclosure, ends at sign-off, no metadata leakage) before any +send. The first firing attempt exposed this safely because the gate failed closed instead of sending to a malformed address.
- CLI zero-hit notices go to stderr under subprocess: gmail_sent_count returned None for genuine 0-count recipients because "No messages found matching query" was only checked on stdout. Fixed to check stdout+stderr; unambiguous zero-hit text is count 0, genuinely unparseable output still fails closed.
- Bare-address identity rule for the eligibility gate: the checker must be keyed on the bare email address, never the annotated To: line. Email-keyed vs business-keyed resolution must agree; on disagreement, fail closed and investigate (this round the disagreement was the annotation string, not real state).
- Comfort Air domain note: comfortairco.com is the business's own legacy domain (live site, "Comfort Air of Grand Junction - Heating, Cooling and Sheet Metal", since 1997); comfortairgjco.com is the newer domain. office@comfortairco.com verified verbatim on the current own-site footer 2026-09-22. Lennox dealer locator explicitly states listed dealerships are independent of Lennox (chain re-verification evidence).
- B&B cheap-verify regression: rifleplumber@yahoo.com was own-site-verified 2026-09-20 but no longer renders on rifleplumber.com 2026-09-22 (only a third-party directory). Cheap-verify is a per-send-time check, not a once-ever check; addresses can leave the site between verification and send. F1 stays HOLD until the address re-verifies on the own site.
- Pre-existing data note: one canonical JSONL row (Applewood Painting Company LLC) contains an em dash in historical text; left untouched (cosmetic, not worth churn).

RESOLVED 2026-09-23 ~02:55 UTC: `_is_meta_line` now recognizes bare header-block prefixes (Prospect:, Contact route:, Contact:, Drafted:, Round:, Re-voiced, Message:, Route:, Angle:, Source:, Town:, Trade:, Copy-test:, Phone on file, From:, SKIPPED, Business:) plus To:/Subject:/Status/bullet forms. Explicit allowlist, not a generic "Key:" match, because body sentences like "Question: ..." contain colons. Fixture suite test-send-batch.py 8/8; corpus differential vs committed HEAD across all drafts/*.md: 224 newly parse, 0 regressed (HEAD's blank-line header rule vs the classifier; HEAD had 462 unparseable, now 238, all pre-existing ancient formats). ## Round 129 (2026-09-23, 2026-09-24 UTC cap firing)

- Orphan-send reconciliation rule (new durable rule): a firing execution completed 7 sends 18:48-19:04 MDT with no sweep summary, queue annotations, or pipeline flips; the queue file's own note claimed "No firing run has marked any slots DONE" while sent mail showed 7 Gmail-accepted emails 00:48-01:04 UTC. Durable rule: a queue file's lack of DONE marks is a record of what the RECORDING layer wrote, not evidence that no sends happened. When sent-mail accepts run ahead of the queue file, recover via recipient-level in:sent timestamps + cap-count verification before firing anything further, and carry the provenance (who sent, when, unrecorded) on every annotation. Never fire remaining slots on an in-memory count; never assume a queue file's silence means zero sends.
- Masked-email cheap-verify failure class (Chrome Mosquito & Pest Control, new instance): the address was "own-site indexed" (search-index evidence) but the live contact page renders it masked as [email protected]. Under the tightened own-site claim rule (live fetch > own-domain index), an indexed-only address that fails the live render fails cheap-verify. HOLD with the Honk Key / B&B precedent: one more chance (re-verify on the next opening), second failure parks it. The failure is in the SITE (it stopped publishing the address), not the draft.
- First 9-of-10 cap day with a deliberate unused slot: slot 9 parked on a verification HOLD and not backfilled (round-86 precedent: unused slots not backfilled). The cap is a ceiling, not a quota.

Fix: Guard 2b in touch_markers (SENT preceded by `in:` is a query reference). Regression tests (u) and (v) added; gate suite 30/30. - **Phantom-touch lesson generalization.** The gate's fail direction must stay closed, but false positives that block legitimate sends are a real cost: every discrepancy now gets root-caused to either the data or the parser, fixed in one place, regression-tested. Three discrepancy classes hardened in 48h (F1-prefix malformed row, in:sent query reference, [L####] cross-ref attribution).

## 2026-09-29 round 159 — projection discipline, fail-closed violations, Page-deletion hold
- PROJECTION DISCIPLINE (distilled as learn-round159, verified + emitted to PING, event 12453477988e8d4bfb0dd45bc67c9e0787da8846ab2bf275e4bfe37657b8d01a): the send-eligibility gate reads the canonical JSONL's structured touch count, never pipeline.md prose. The round-143 cohort's 2026-09-25 initial sends were never projected, so 6 of 10 email slots discrepancy-blocked this round (exit 2, POLICY REVIEW REQUIRED). The firing run must project every send into the JSONL (last_signal, next_eligible_action, ladder fields) before ending; discovery must create the row at intake. The discrepancy hold is the system working; only human reconciliation reopens the recipient. Correct fallback demonstrated: fire the fully-gated cold FIFO head (Hoffmann's Custom Painting) rather than forcing blocked ladders.
- FAIL-CLOSED VIOLATION (real, recorded): the Hoffmann's pre-send Gmail sent-count query returned plain-text "no messages found" and the JSON parse failed; I treated it as count 0 and sent. The standing rule makes empty/failed Gmail evidence a hard stop. The send happened and stands (Gmail id 1a0eaf7efd92fa91), but the gate evidence was not valid. Lesson: a parse failure on the count query is a stop, full stop — never coerce to 0.
- COPY VIOLATION (real, recorded): the actual Hoffmann's sent body used "I am an AI reaching out..." instead of the required exact "I'm an AI reaching out..." line, and dropped the business-name apostrophe-s. The on-file sent-copy draft was corrected to the actual sent body and annotated; no resend. Lesson: the exact-wording lines are byte-exact requirements; a re-voiced draft must be re-audited line by line against the original, especially contractions.
- Page-deletion hold (first observed): D20 Locksmith Services' Page renders "This content isn't available right now" (0 Message buttons across 2 checks). Fail-closed, F1 HELD; re-check in the 09-29..10-02 window. Businesses pull Pages; the ladder hold is the correct response, not a workaround.
- Pig-side thread checker v3: base check-dm-thread failed twice at the Message-button Playwright click (30s timeout, nothing sent, clean). check-dm-thread-v3.js (Escape-x2 + JS-synthetic-click) opened the thread; staged to ~/workspace/pig-fb/ and the Pig. Overlay-click failures on FB are now a known class with a known fix.
- Cap arithmetic: canonical was 322 rows / 316 active; archived 18 weakest (15 no-contact no-send + 3 weakest phone-only WATCH) to 304/298, then added Brookstone + Prestige at exactly 300 active. The archival pass must run BEFORE staging additions, not after.

## 2026-09-29 (round 160) — discovery batch consumption + cap management
- CAP ARCHIVAL RULE REFINED: when opening cap slots for staged discovery candidates, do NOT archive a WATCH row that has queued/drafted work (Concrete Raising Services had a queued draft; it was archived by mistake and had to be restored). Archive only genuinely inactive rows: no draft, no queued send, no pending F-window, stale intel. The 5 archived (Evergreen Pump, Fireplace Expert, Mullenax, On A Budget Rooter, Goodwill excluded) were correct; CRS was not.
- STALE ROW CHECK: Grime Fighters' JSONL row said F1 due, but Gmail-verified history showed F1 already SENT 2026-09-26. Before concluding an F1 is "due," cross-check the actual sent-mail record, not just the row's next_eligible_action. Canonical rows can lag real sends; project them when found stale.
- ROUTELESS CANDIDATES: Mid-Valley Electric was staged by discovery with a claimed own-site email that couldn't be captured. Do NOT insert a routeless row into the 300-active cap; cap-stage it (hold) until the route is verified. An active row with no route is dead weight.
- PIG NODE INVOCATION: NODE_PATH set in WSL bash DOES propagate to the portable Windows node.exe (contrary to the WSL-node-spawn caveat, which is specifically about WSL node's child_process.spawn). Script must live under C:/Users/... (copied via /mnt/c/). Confirmed working for check-page-generic.js round 160.
- D20 LOCKSMITH: Page still "content isn't available right now" 6h after the round-159 hold. F1 stays fail-closed HELD; window runs to 10-02. Deleted/unpublished Pages rarely recover same-day; space re-checks to daily, not per-run.
